## Math, including an aligned environment and math inside a list

$$
\begin{aligned}
\nabla \cdot \mathbf{E} &= \frac{\rho}{\varepsilon_0} \\
\nabla \times \mathbf{B} &= \mu_0 \mathbf{J} + \mu_0 \varepsilon_0 \frac{\partial \mathbf{E}}{\partial t}
\end{aligned}
$$

- A list item containing display math:
  $$
  \int_0^\infty e^{-x^2} dx = \frac{\sqrt{\pi}}{2}
  $$
- And a second item, to keep the first honest.
